### ⚖️⛔⛔⛔⭐⭐⭐ THE RULING ONLY THE MANAGER MAKES — **(A) ONE PATH, ⛔ NOT (B) A PARALLEL AGENT LANE. ⛔ WRITTEN HERE, ⛔ IN ADVANCE, ⛔ BEFORE ONE LINE IS SPECCED.**

- ⛔ **(A) = the agent drives the ⛔ SAME site the player drives** — one predicate, one handler, one trace; the only thing that differs is ⛔ where two screen-space numbers came from. ⇒ ⛔ **a green verdict then means something.**
- ⛔ **(B) = a parallel agent-only entry point** (an exec / `BlueprintCallable` `AgentConfirmPlacementAt(FVector)`, an "agent confirm" branch, a second resize path). ⛔ **Cheap, fast, and it ⛔ PROVES LESS THAN IT APPEARS TO: its green attests to a path ⛔ NO PLAYER EVER TAKES.**
- 🚨⛔⛔ **THIS PROJECT ALREADY HAS THE SCAR AND IT IS ⛔ NAMED, ⛔ NOT ALLUDED TO: ⭐ `TASK-1286` shipped past ⛔ TWO QA gates, a ⛔ CLEAN COMPILE and a ⛔ 561/561 SUITE ⛔ WHILE UNABLE TO EXECUTE A LINE OF ITS ENTRY PATH; and on `L_MainMenu` the `IA_` route the rig ⛔ CAN drive is ⛔ INERT IN 🧑 HIS HANDS because `FInputModeUIOnly` calls `SetIgnoreInput(true)`** (⭐ `VER-§5` cl. 5 · ⭐ `VER-§8` cl. 1). ⇒ ⚖️ ***⛔ THERE, ⛔ "VERIFIABLE" AND ⛔ "REAL" ARE ⛔ ACTIVELY OPPOSED. ⛔ A SHAPE THAT OPTIMISES FOR THE FIRST ⛔ BUYS NOTHING FOR THE SECOND.***
- ✅⛔⛔ **THEREFORE, ⛔ STANDING FOR THIS WHOLE WAVE: ⛔ EVERY IMPLEMENTATION ROW ⭐ `TASK-1350` BOARDS TAKES SHAPE ⛔ (A). ⛔ SHAPE (B) IS ⛔ REFUSED IN ADVANCE, ⛔ BY NAME, AND A ROW THAT ARRIVES CARRYING ONE IS A ⛔ SCOPE BLOCKER (⭐ `SC-§100`), ⛔ NOT A DESIGN DISCUSSION.** ⛔ **IF ⛔ ANY ROW IS ⛔ LATER FORCED TO (B) BY A ⛔ MEASURED IMPOSSIBILITY, ⛔ THE ROW ⛔ SAYS ON ITS OWN FACE ⛔ WHAT ITS GREEN VERDICT DOES ⛔ NOT ATTEST TO, ⛔ AND 🧑 HE IS TOLD IT IS A ⛔ DEVIATION FROM HIS STATED GOAL — ⛔ named, ⛔ never buried** (⭐ `SC-§121` cl. 3: ⛔ an option whose label omits a decision inside it is ⛔ not a clean ask).

### 🚨⛔⛔ CAPABILITY CEILING — **DECLARED HERE, AT ⛔ BOARDING, FOR THE ⛔ WHOLE WAVE (⭐ `VER-§8` cl. 2). ⛔ DISCOVERING ANY OF THESE AT 5b IS A ⛔ MANAGER DEFECT.**

1. ⛔ **THE OS CURSOR IS ⛔ NOT MOVABLE BY ANY GRANTED TOOL.** `simulate_key_press "LeftMouseButton"` ⛔ DOES reach the raw poll; ⛔ the ⛔ TRACE then fails because the cursor is ⛔ not over the PIE window and ⛔ `ui_perform` ⛔ RESTORES it. ⛔ **Measured across 3 camera pitches, ⛔ refused 3×.** ⇒ 🚨 ⛔ **THE BLOCKER IS THE ⛔ AIM POINT, ⛔ NOT THE BUTTON. ⛔ ANY ROW THAT SPECS *"send a click"* AND ⛔ NOT *"what does the trace hit?"* HAS ⛔ SPECCED THE EASY HALF.**
2. ⛔ **SLATE IS UNREACHABLE.** `simulate_key_press` is delivered to the ⛔ player controller / Enhanced Input and ⛔ NOT to Slate (⭐ `VER-§8` cl. 1, measured ⛔ twice with a firing control pair). ⛔ UMG button clicks are ⛔ `UNOBSERVABLE` ⛔ BY CONSTRUCTION (⭐ `VER-§5` cl. 5).
3. ⛔ **`L_MainMenu` IS A ⛔ NAMED DEAD END** (`applied_mapping_contexts: []`; `ui_perform`'s three click shapes ⛔ never fire `OnClicked`). ⇒ ⛔ **every probe and every acceptance in this wave runs ⛔ ROUTE B: `load_level L_Arena` direct, vs-bot.** ⛔ Nobody re-traces the menu.
4. ⛔ **NO NEW ENGINE TOOL AND NO NEW MCP LANE MAY BE GRANTED TO FIX ANY OF THIS** — ⛔ that is an ⛔ AURA-SIDE ask, ⛔ not ours (⭐ `VER-§7` cl. 1/2/3: ⛔ never wholesale, ⛔ enumerated-or-not-at-all). ⛔ A row that proposes one is ⛔ refused and ⛔ routed to 🧑 him as an ask.
5. ⛔ **`SC-§129` DOES ⛔ NOT FIRE ON THIS WAVE, AND THAT IS ⛔ STATED RATHER THAN ASSUMED (⭐ `SC-§70`): ⛔ NO ROW HERE READS, WRITES OR RUNS `Tools/run_suite_bounded.ps1`.** ⛔ If ⭐ `TASK-1350` later boards a row that does, ⛔ that row carries `SC-§129` ⛔ and this line does not cover it.
6. ⛔ **THE BOT KILLS AN UNDRIVEN HERO AT ≈`t=2m50s` on Route B** (⭐ `VER-§6` cl. 6) ⇒ ⛔ **every probe's observable must land ⛔ before `t=90 s` or PIE restarts.** ⛔ Budget accordingly; ⛔ do not discover it mid-run.

    🚨⭐⭐ **(B1) ⛔ THE ⛔ LOAD-BEARING LINE — ⛔ THE ⛔ SET-AND-ACT RECIPE. ⛔ DOES `SetMouseLocation(X,Y)` FOLLOWED ⛔ IMMEDIATELY BY THE DEPENDENT READ, ⛔ INSIDE ⛔ ONE BATCHED `run_verification_sequence`, PRODUCE A ⛔ REPRODUCIBLE WORLD POINT?** ⛔ **METHOD: the ⛔ SAME `(X,Y)` driven ⛔ ≥3 times, ⛔ each time as `set → read` in ⛔ ONE batch, at a camera you ⛔ prove byte-identical before and after.** ⛔ **THE QUESTION IS ⛔ SPREAD, ⛔ NOT PRESENCE: ⛔ quote the ⛔ three world points and their ⛔ pairwise distances. ⛔ ⭐ `TASK-1352`'s ⛔ UNBATCHED repeat spread ⛔ ≈648 uu — ⛔ THAT IS YOUR BASELINE TO BEAT and ⛔ you quote it.** ⛔ **THE CONTROL, ⛔ AND IT IS WHAT MAKES ANY NUMBER MEAN ANYTHING: ⛔ the ⛔ SAME batch with the ⛔ SET OMITTED ⇒ ⛔ it must ⛔ NOT produce the same point.** ⇒ 🚨 ⛔ **IF B1 LANDS, ⛔ SAY SO IN ONE SENTENCE AT THE TOP — ⛔ it is the sentence that ⛔ BRINGS DOWN ⭐ `VER-§8` cl. 7's third ceiling, and ⛔ that is the manager's to write, ⛔ not yours.**
    ⛔ **(B2) ⛔ H1 FROM ⭐ `TASK-1352`, ⛔ TESTED ⛔ NOT ASSUMED: ⛔ does `bShowMouseCursor: true` make the point ⛔ HOLD?** ⛔ **METHOD: `set_actor_property` `bShowMouseCursor` → `true` (⛔ a lane ⭐ `TASK-1352`'s A2 ⛔ PROVED ALIVE), ⛔ then `SetMouseLocation`, ⛔ then read the observable at ⛔ ≥2 s.** ⛔ **CONTROL: the ⛔ SAME sequence with `bShowMouseCursor` left at its ⛔ MEASURED RESTING VALUE `false`.** 🚨⛔ **⛔ THIS IS A ⛔ PROBE-ONLY WRITE AND YOU ⛔ REVERT IT ⛔ BEFORE THE SESSION ENDS. ⛔ IT IS ⛔ NOT A PROPOSAL TO SHIP `bShowMouseCursor: true` — ⛔ that would put a ⛔ VISIBLE CURSOR in a player's match, ⛔ a player-facing change ⛔ NOBODY ASKED FOR, ⛔ and it is ⛔ REFUSED BY NAME HERE so it cannot arrive later wearing a measurement.**

    ⛔ **(1) ⛔ CHECK ONE — ⛔ DID THE CONTROLS ⛔ FIRE? ⛔ For ⛔ EVERY claim-making acceptance line, ⛔ name the control and say ⛔ WHETHER IT COULD HAVE FAILED.** ⛔ **A ⛔ GREEN CHECK THAT ⛔ CANNOT FAIL IS ⛔ EXACTLY WHAT THIS ROW EXISTS TO PREVENT (⭐ `SC-§137`).** ⛔ **B1's control is the ⛔ SET-OMITTED batch; ⛔ B2's is the ⛔ `false` arm; ⛔ B3's is the ⛔ SAME-SESSION pitch-0 repeat; ⛔ B4's is the ⛔ log line ⛔ FIRING at least once.** ⛔ **⛔ A control taken in a ⛔ DIFFERENT SESSION is ⛔ NOT a control — ⛔ say so if you find one.**
    🚨⛔ **(2) ⛔ CHECK TWO — ⛔ IS ANY ⛔ NEGATIVE ⛔ INCOMPLETE? ⛔ THIS IS THE ⛔ WARN-B CHECK AND IT IS ⛔ THE REASON THIS ROW IS BOARDED.** ⛔ **If the report concludes *"the recipe does not work"*, ⛔ enumerate what it ⛔ ACTUALLY TRIED against what the ⛔ lane makes available, and say whether the negative is ⛔ COMPLETE or ⛔ MERELY UNENUMERATED. ⛔ THOSE ARE ⛔ NOT THE SAME WORD — ⛔ and last time the difference was ⛔ one permanent forever-cost.**
    ⛔ **(3) ⛔ CHECK THREE — ⛔ THE ⛔ SPREAD ARITHMETIC. ⛔ B1's claim is about ⛔ DISTANCE, ⛔ not presence. ⛔ RE-COMPUTE the pairwise distances ⛔ from the quoted coordinates ⛔ YOURSELF. ⛔ A spread the report ⛔ asserts but does ⛔ not show its arithmetic for is an ⛔ empty cell.**
    ⛔ **(4) ⛔ CHECK FOUR — ⛔ CELL TOKENS against ⭐ `VER-§1` cl. 3a: ⛔ is the ⛔ FIRST WORD of every cell one of `pass` / `fail` / `unobs` / `measured`? ⛔ Is any ⛔ CONTROLLED negative mis-scored `fail` (⛔ which would ⛔ mechanically derive `VERIFY-FAILED` and ⛔ bounce a row with ⛔ no code)? ⛔ Is any ⛔ UNCONTROLLED negative scored `measured` rather than `unobs` (⛔ the ⛔ anti-abuse limb — ⛔ `measured` is ⛔ EARNED BY THE CONTROL)?**
    ⛔ **(5) ⛔ CHECK FIVE — ⛔ B2's REVERT. ⛔ Did the row ⛔ REVERT `bShowMouseCursor`, and is the revert ⛔ SHOWN BY A READ-BACK rather than ⛔ asserted? ⛔ A probe write left live in the editor is a ⛔ finding.**

    ⛔ **(7) ⛔ ANYTHING LAW-SHAPED YOU FIND: ⛔ FLAG IT, ⛔ DO NOT WRITE IT (⭐ `SC-§82`). ⛔ ⭐ `TASK-1349` §7 did ⛔ exactly this with `MEASURED` — ⛔ endorsed it, ⛔ declined to mint it, ⛔ and the manager minted it the same day. ⛔ That is the shape.**
